&= \dfrac{24120} {1000} \\\\
&= 24.12
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E172D520-4089-4F05-926E-DB8874B70DB6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$20.1 \div 6 =$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20.1 \div 6  \\\\
&= \dfrac{20.1}{6}  \\\\
&= \dfrac{20.1 \times 100}{6 \times 100}    \\\\
&= \dfrac{2010}{6 \times 100}   \\\\
&= \dfrac{6\times 335}{6 \times 100}   \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{6}\times 335}{\cancel{6} \times 100}   \\\\
&= \dfrac{335}{100} \\\\
&= 3.35
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3.35$

</div>
</div>

&= \dfrac{6\times 335}{6 \times 100}   \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{6}\times 335}{\cancel{6} \times 100}   \\\\
&= \dfrac{335}{100} \\\\

\end{aligned}

<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20.11 \times 1.1 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2011 \times 11} {100 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2011 \times 11} {1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {22121} {1000} \\\\
&= 22.121
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

$22.121$

&= 22.121
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

&= \dfrac{\cancel{19} \times \cancel{25} \times 25}{\cancel{19} \times \cancel{25}}  \\\\
&= 25
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-99068888-BD24-4A8E-8D23-A75AB41224E3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the smallest number that $2, 3$ and $4$ all divide into exactly? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

The smallest number that  all divide into exactly is the LCM of the numbers.


LCM of $2$, $3$ and $4 = 12$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_lcm_note
<emptyline>
LCM of $2$, $3$ and $4 = 12$.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$12$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write down a $\underline{fraction}$ between $0.5$ and $0.6$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {0.5 + 0.6} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1.1} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1.1 \times 10} {2 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11} {20}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {11} {20}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {0.5 + 0.6} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1.1} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1.1 \times 10} {2 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11} {20}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {11} {20}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write down a $\underline{decimal}$ between $\dfrac{3}{4}$ and $1$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (\dfrac {3} {4} + 1) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (0.75 + 1) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 1.75 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1.75} {2} \\\\
&= 0.875
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.875$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (\dfrac {3} {4} + 1) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (0.75 + 1) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 1.75 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1.75} {2} \\\\
&= 0.875
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.875$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$\dfrac{1}{4} = ………….$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {100} \\\\
&= 0.25
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$

<div class='question question'>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20.02 \times 1.5 \\\\\
&= \dfrac {2002 \times 15} {100 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2002 \times 15} {1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {30030} {1000} \\\\
&= 30.03
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$30.03$

</div>
</div>

&= 20.02 \times 1.5 \\\\\
&= \dfrac {2002 \times 15} {100 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2002 \times 15} {1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {30030} {1000} \\\\
&= 30.03
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 600$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 6 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 10 \times 6 \\\\
&= \pounds 60
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 60$

</div>

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class='working'>

Let's first list down other percentages between $75\%$ and $80\%$ and then convert the same into decimal.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 75 \%, \quad \underline{76\%}, \quad \underline{77\%}, \quad \underline{78\%}, \quad \underline{79\%}, \quad 80\%
\end{aligned}
$

Let's convert 76\% into decimal.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {76} {100}\\\\
&= 0.76
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write down a decimal between $75\%$ and $80\%$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {75\% + 80\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155\%} {2} \\\\
&= 77.5\% \\\\
&= \dfrac {77.5} {100} \\\\
&= 0.775
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.76 \ \text{or} \ 0.775$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {75\% + 80\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155\%} {2} \\\\
&= 77.5\% \\\\
&= \dfrac {77.5} {100} \\\\
&= 0.775
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write down a fraction between $60\%$ and $70\%$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {60\% + 70\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {130\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {130} {100 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {130} {200} \\\\
&= \dfrac {130} {200} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 13} {10 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{10} \times 13} {\cancel{10} \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13} {20}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {13} {20}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {60\% + 70\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {130\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {130} {100 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {130} {200} \\\\
&= \dfrac {130} {200} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 13} {10 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{10} \times 13} {\cancel{10} \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13} {20}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C5B51626-C8B8-4D6E-9DF1-29BB79106574'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Add together the following, giving your answer as a DECIMAL

$65\%, 0.507$ and $\dfrac{1}{4}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 65\% + 0.507 + \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {65} {100} + 0.507 + \dfrac {25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
&= 0.65 + 0.507 + \dfrac {25} {100} \\\\
&= 0.65 + 0.507 + 0.25 \\\\
&= 1.407
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.407$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 65\% + 0.507 + \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {65} {100} + 0.507 + \dfrac {25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
&= 0.65 + 0.507 + \dfrac {25} {100} \\\\
&= 0.65 + 0.507 + 0.25 \\\\
&= 1.407
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1.407$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 2} {5 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {10} \\\\
&= 0.2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

$0.2$

&= 0.2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

$0.06$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {50} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2} {50 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6} {100} \\\\
&= 0.06
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

What is $\text{two}$ and $\text{thirty four hundredths}$ when it is written as a decimal?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$2.34$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2.34$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.34$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7D42A72B-3C61-447C-BB29-F28D7B583B43'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of these numbers is NOT a multiple of $3$?

$12\:\: 234\:\: 3456\:\: 45678\:\: 567890$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As we know that a number is divisible by 3, if the sum of all its digits is divisible by 3.


Using that,

| Number    |         Sum                                           | Multiple of $3$
|:--------: |:----------------------------------------------------: | :-----:
|  $12$     | $1 + 2 = \red{3}$                                     |  Yes
|  $234$    | $2 + 3 + 4 = \red{9}$                                 |  Yes
|  $3456$   | $3 + 4 + 5 + 6 = \red{15}$                            |  Yes
|  $45678$  | $4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + 8 = \red{30}$                        |  Yes
|  $567890$ | $5 + 6 + 7 + 8 + 9 + 0 = \green{35}$                  |  No


Not a multiple of $3 = 567890$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$567890$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_divisible_by_3
<emptyline>
Using that,
<emptyline>
| Number    |         Sum                                           | Multiple of $3$
|:--------: |:----------------------------------------------------: | :-----:
|  $12$     | $1 + 2 = \red{3}$                                     |  Yes
|  $234$    | $2 + 3 + 4 = \red{9}$                                 |  Yes
|  $3456$   | $3 + 4 + 5 + 6 = \red{15}$                            |  Yes
|  $45678$  | $4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + 8 = \red{30}$                        |  Yes
|  $567890$ | $5 + 6 + 7 + 8 + 9 + 0 = \green{35}$                  |  No
<emptyline>

Not a multiple of $3 = 567890$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$567890$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8A105B27-7161-49B8-9344-3AAE3778D055'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What does $2 \times 17 + 3 \times 17 + 5 \times 17 =$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \times 17 + 3 \times 17 + 5 \times 17 \\\\
&= (2 + 3 + 5) \times 17 \\\\
&= 10 \times 17 \\\\
&= 170
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$170$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \times 17 + 3 \times 17 + 5 \times 17 \\\\
&= (2 + 3 + 5) \times 17 \\\\
&= 10 \times 17 \\\\
&= 170
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CC7E5372-5CB7-4E8F-9082-1AE6E8B336FD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the difference between the largest and smallest of these numbers

$0.89\:\: 0.9\:\: 0.17\:\: 0.72\:\: 0.73$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Largest number}                   &= 0.9 \\\\
\text{Smallest number}                  &= 0.17 \\\\
\text{Difference}                       &= 0.9 - 0.17 \\\\
                                        &= 0.73
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.73$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Largest number}                   &= 0.9 \\\\
\text{Smallest number}                  &= 0.17 \\\\
\text{Difference}                       &= 0.9 - 0.17 \\\\
                                        &= 0.73
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-89CBFDE4-945C-49CB-88BA-378290DC6C26'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Use your previous answer to write down $2030 \times 5$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using our previous answer

$2030 \times 20 = 40600$

$2030 \times 5 \times 4 = 40600$

$2030 \times 5 = \dfrac {40600} {4}$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2030 \times 5 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2030 \times 5 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2030 \times 20} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40600} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10150 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10150 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 10150
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$10150$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using our previous answer
<emptyline>
$2030 \times 20 = 40600$
<emptyline>
$2030 \times 5 \times 4 = 40600$
<emptyline>
$2030 \times 5 = \dfrac {40600} {4}$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2030 \times 5 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2030 \times 5 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2030 \times 20} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40600} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10150 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10150 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 10150
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$10150$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-42073645-B85D-44D2-B12C-2CD2DF1CD58F'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Use your previous answer to write down

$20.30 × 2.5$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20.3 \times 2.5 \\\\
&= \dfrac{2030} {100} \times \dfrac{25} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2030 \times 25} {100 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{50750} {1000} \\\\
&= 50.75
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$50.75$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20.3 \times 2.5 \\\\
&= \dfrac{2030} {100} \times \dfrac{25} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2030 \times 25} {100 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{50750} {1000} \\\\
&= 50.75
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  500 \times 1.5 \\\\
&=  \dfrac{500 \times 1.5 \times 10}{10} \\\\
&=  \dfrac{500 \times 1.5 \times \cancel{10}}{\cancel{10}} \\\\
&=  500 \times 1.5 \\\\
&=  750 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7F7BEB1F-1352-462D-862B-8AEFB56B58F6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Write down a decimal between $90\%$ and $95\%$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's first list down other percentages between $90\%$ and $95\%$ and then convert the same into decimal.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 90 \%, \quad \underline{91\%}, \quad \underline{92\%}, \quad \underline{93\%}, \quad \underline{94\%}, \quad 95\%
\end{aligned}
$

Let's convert 91\% into decimal.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {91} {100}\\\\
&= 0.91
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Alternate solution,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {90\% + 95\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {185\%} {2} \\\\
&= 92.5\% \\\\
&= \dfrac {92.5} {100} \\\\
&= 0.925
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.91 \ \text{or} \ 0.925$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's first list down other percentages between $90\%$ and $95\%$ and then convert the same into decimal.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 90 \%, \quad \underline{91\%}, \quad \underline{92\%}, \quad \underline{93\%}, \quad \underline{94\%}, \quad 95\%
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's convert 91\% into decimal.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {91} {100}\\\\
&= 0.91
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Alternate solution,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {90\% + 95\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {185\%} {2} \\\\
&= 92.5\% \\\\
&= \dfrac {92.5} {100} \\\\
&= 0.925
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write down a decimal between $80\%$ and $90\%$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {80\% + 90\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {170\%} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {170} {100 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {170} {200} \\\\
&= \dfrac {170 \times 5} {200 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {850} {1000} \\\\
&= 0.85
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.85$

&= \dfrac {170 \times 5} {200 \times 5} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A4A02C1D-8BBA-436D-A1CF-94DD32988031'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Add together the following, giving your answer as a DECIMAL

$\dfrac {2} {5}, \ 35\%$ and $0.604$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {5} + 35\% + 0.604 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {5 \times 2} + \dfrac {35} {100} + 0.604 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {10} + 0.35 + 0.604 \\\\
&= 0.4 + 0.35 + 0.604 \\\\
&= 1.354
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.354$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {5} + 35\% + 0.604 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {5 \times 2} + \dfrac {35} {100} + 0.604 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {10} + 0.35 + 0.604 \\\\
&= 0.4 + 0.35 + 0.604 \\\\
&= 1.354
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1.354$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write $62\%$ as a fraction in its lowest terms.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 62\% \\\\
&= \dfrac {62} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 31} {2 \times 50} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 31} {\cancel{2} \times 50} \\\\
&= \dfrac {31} {50}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {31} {50}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 62\% \\\\
&= \dfrac {62} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 31} {2 \times 50} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 31} {\cancel{2} \times 50} \\\\
&= \dfrac {31} {50}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {31} {50}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write $\dfrac{1}{8}$ as a decimal.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 125} {8 \times 125} \\\\
&= \dfrac {125} {1000} \\\\
&= 0.125
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

&= 0.125
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $5371 \div 131?$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5371 \div 131 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5371} {131} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5371 \times 41} {131 \times 41} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5371 \times 41} {5371} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel {5371} \times 41} {\cancel {5371}} \\\\
&= 41
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$41$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5371 \div 131 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5371} {131} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5371 \times 41} {131 \times 41} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5371 \times 41} {5371} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel {5371} \times 41} {\cancel {5371}} \\\\
&= 41
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

What is $4.1 \times 13.1$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4.1 \times 13.1 \\\\
&= \dfrac{41} {10} \times \dfrac{131} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{41 \times 131} {10 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5371} {100} \\\\
&= 53.71
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C5396C65-8D48-4EC7-B2F5-B976D05267E8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of these is the largest number?

$2+0+1+8$

$2\times0+1+8$

$2+0\times1+8$

$2+0+1\times8$

$2\times0\times1\times8$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Calculating all options

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{A}    &= 2 + 0 + 1 + 8 \\\\
            &= 11 \\\\
\text{B}    &= 2 \times 0 + 1 + 8 \\\\
            &= 0 + 1 + 8 \\\\
            &= 9 \\\\
\text{C}    &= 2 + 0 \times 1 + 8 \\\\
            &= 2 + 0 + 8 \\\\
            &= 10 \\\\
\text{D}    &= 2 + 0 + 1 \times 8 \\\\
            &= 2 + 0 + 8 \\\\
            &= 10 \\\\
\text{E}    &= 2\times0\times1\times8 \\\\
            &= 0
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2+0+1+8$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Calculating all options
<emptyline>
rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{A}    &= 2 + 0 + 1 + 8 \\\\
            &= 11 \\\\
\text{B}    &= 2 \times 0 + 1 + 8 \\\\
            &= 0 + 1 + 8 \\\\
            &= 9 \\\\
\text{C}    &= 2 + 0 \times 1 + 8 \\\\
            &= 2 + 0 + 8 \\\\
            &= 10 \\\\
\text{D}    &= 2 + 0 + 1 \times 8 \\\\
            &= 2 + 0 + 8 \\\\
            &= 10 \\\\
\text{E}    &= 2\times0\times1\times8 \\\\
            &= 0
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2+0+1+8$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
